# Supporting types

Generated by `bun run docs:protocol`. Edit source schemas, then regenerate.

JSON Schema validates structure. `x-ace-constraint` names additional semantic rules that clients must enforce; Zod remains the full validator.

## AccountAssignment

[JSON Schema](schema/AccountAssignment.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| instanceId | no | [AccountInstanceId.json](schema/AccountInstanceId.json) |  |
| role | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":64} |
| estimatedLoad | yes | number | {"minimum":0,"maximum":100} |

Example:

```json
{
  "estimatedLoad": 0,
  "role": "example"
}
```

## AccountBadgeInput

[JSON Schema](schema/AccountBadgeInput.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: One or two non-whitespace text graphemes, or one emoji/icon grapheme, at most 64 UTF-16 code units.

Type: string. See JSON Schema for constraints.

Example:

```json
"👩‍💻"
```

## AccountDirectory

[JSON Schema](schema/AccountDirectory.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: Absolute Unix or drive-qualified Windows directory.

Type: string. See JSON Schema for constraints.

Example:

```json
"/example/home"
```

## AccountQuota

[JSON Schema](schema/AccountQuota.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| auth | yes | ["logged_in","logged_out","unknown"] |  |
| cursorSdkAuth | no | [CursorSdkAuth.json](schema/CursorSdkAuth.json) |  |
| observedAt | yes | number | {"minimum":0} |
| billingMode | no | ["api","subscription","unknown"] |  |
| plan | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":128} |
| windows | yes | object | {"propertyNames":{"type":"string","maxLength":128},"additionalProperties":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/QuotaWindow.json"},"maxProperties":32,"x-ace-constraint":"At most 32 quota windows."} |
| blockers | no | object | {"default":{},"properties":{"overflow":{"type":"boolean","const":true},"limitError":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/QuotaWindow.json"},"homeUnavailable":{"type":"string","enum":["home_missing","home_unreadable","foreign_home","home_conflict"]}}} |
| usage | yes | object | {"properties":{"inputTokens":{"type":"number","minimum":0},"outputTokens":{"type":"number","minimum":0},"costUsd":{"type":"number","minimum":0}}} |

Example:

```json
{
  "auth": "logged_out",
  "billingMode": "api",
  "cursorSdkAuth": {
    "source": "environment",
    "status": "logged-out"
  },
  "observedAt": 7,
  "usage": {
    "costUsd": 9,
    "inputTokens": 7,
    "outputTokens": 8
  },
  "windows": {}
}
```

## AccountSummary

[JSON Schema](schema/AccountSummary.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| cliHome | no | string | {"maxLength":4096} |
| implicit | no | boolean |  |
| isDefault | no | boolean |  |
| id | yes | [AccountInstanceId.json](schema/AccountInstanceId.json) |  |
| acpAgentId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| installationId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| instanceId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| homeStrategy | no | `"default_cli"` |  |
| isolation | no | `"unsupported"` |  |
| authMethod | no | ["browser","api_key","unknown"] |  |
| signedInAs | no | string | {"maxLength":256} |
| loginRevision | no | string | {"maxLength":128} |
| profileRevision | no | string | {"maxLength":256} |
| installationVersion | no | string | {"maxLength":256} |
| provider | yes | [AccountProvider.json](schema/AccountProvider.json) |  |
| label | yes | string | {"maxLength":128} |
| shortLabel | no | [AccountShortLabel.json](schema/AccountShortLabel.json) |  |
| badgeUsesInitial | no | boolean |  |
| badgeColor | no | [AccountBadgeColor.json](schema/AccountBadgeColor.json) |  |
| quota | yes | [AccountQuota.json](schema/AccountQuota.json) |  |
| availability | yes | [AccountAvailability.json](schema/AccountAvailability.json) |  |
| blockedUntil | no | union | {"anyOf":[{"type":"number","minimum":0},{"type":"null"}]} |

Example:

```json
{
  "acpAgentId": "example",
  "availability": "exhausted",
  "id": "example",
  "installationVersion": "example",
  "isDefault": false,
  "isolation": "unsupported",
  "label": "example",
  "loginRevision": "example",
  "profileRevision": "example",
  "provider": "opencode",
  "quota": {
    "auth": "logged_in",
    "billingMode": "unknown",
    "cursorSdkAuth": {
      "source": "none",
      "status": "logged-in"
    },
    "observedAt": 5,
    "plan": "example",
    "usage": {
      "outputTokens": 1
    },
    "windows": {}
  }
}
```

## AcpSessionSupport

[JSON Schema](schema/AcpSessionSupport.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| capabilities | yes | object | {"properties":{"resume":{"type":"boolean"},"imageInput":{"type":"boolean"},"planMode":{"type":"boolean"}},"required":["resume","imageInput","planMode"]} |
| mcp | yes | ["http","stdio","unavailable"] |  |
| modelSelection | yes | boolean |  |
| modeSelection | yes | boolean |  |
| subagentSessions | no | boolean | {"default":false} |
| coverage | yes | ["source_profile","generic","legacy"] |  |
| visibility | yes | `"limited"` |  |
| raw | yes | object | {"properties":{"json":{"type":"string","maxLength":2048},"truncated":{"type":"boolean"}},"required":["json","truncated"]} |

Example:

```json

  "capabilities": {
    "imageInput": true,
    "planMode": false,
    "resume": true
  },
  "coverage": "generic",
  "mcp": "unavailable",
  "modeSelection": false,
  "modelSelection": true,
  "raw": {
    "json": "example",
    "truncated": false
  },
  "visibility": "limited"
}
```

| native | yes | [NativeRef.json](schema/NativeRef.json) |  |
| fidelity | yes | [AgentFidelity.json](schema/AgentFidelity.json) |  |
| spawnedBy | no | union | {"anyOf":[{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ItemId.json"},{"type":"null"}]} |
| name | no | string |  |
| role | no | string |  |
| model | no | string |  |
| cwd | yes | string |  |
| status | yes | [AgentStatus.json](schema/AgentStatus.json) |  |
| background | no | boolean | {"default":false} |
| createdAt | yes | [Timestamp.json](schema/Timestamp.json) |  |
| endedAt | no | [Timestamp.json](schema/Timestamp.json) |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "background": true,
  "createdAt": 0,
  "cwd": "example",
  "endedAt": 8,
  "fidelity": "summary",
  "id": "example",
  "model": "example",
  "name": "example",
  "native": {
    "aliases": [],
    "installationId": "example",
    "instanceId": "example",
    "nativeId": "example",
    "provider": "pi"
  },
  "origin": "ace",
  "parentId": null,
  "status": {
    "state": "starting"
  },
  "threadId": "example"
}
```

### Variant 28

Semantic rule: Exactly one of url or number is required.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| op | yes | `"thread.link_pr"` |  |
| url | no | string | {"format":"ace-whatwg-url","x-ace-url-maxLength":2048,"x-ace-constraint":"Accept strings whose trimmed value is an absolute WHATWG URL. Whitespace, Unicode hosts, opaque schemes and parser-normalized URLs are allowed. x-ace-url-minLength and x-ace-url-maxLength apply after trimming whitespace and removing ASCII tab, CR and LF characters, using UTF-16 code units. Install the ace-whatwg-url format validator or enforce this rule in application code."} |
| number | no | integer | {"exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| repo | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":512} |

Example:

```json
{
  "op": "thread.link_pr",
  "url": "https://example.invalid/"
}
```

### Variant 29

Semantic rule: Exactly one of url, number, or all: true is required.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| op | yes | `"thread.unlink_pr"` |  |
| url | no | string | {"format":"ace-whatwg-url","x-ace-url-maxLength":2048,"x-ace-constraint":"Accept strings whose trimmed value is an absolute WHATWG URL. Whitespace, Unicode hosts, opaque schemes and parser-normalized URLs are allowed. x-ace-url-minLength and x-ace-url-maxLength apply after trimming whitespace and removing ASCII tab, CR and LF characters, using UTF-16 code units. Install the ace-whatwg-url format validator or enforce this rule in application code."} |
| number | no | integer | {"exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| all | no | `true` |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "number": 5,
  "op": "thread.unlink_pr"
}
```

## BrowserFrame

[JSON Schema](schema/BrowserFrame.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| sequence | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| timestamp | yes | number |  |
| data | yes | string | {"maxLength":4194304} |
| width | yes | integer | {"minimum":100,"maximum":4096} |
| height | yes | integer | {"minimum":100,"maximum":4096} |

Example:

```json
{
  "data": "example",
  "height": 109,
  "sequence": 2,
  "timestamp": 2,
  "width": 106
}
```

## BrowserInput

[JSON Schema](schema/BrowserInput.json), input validation.

### mouse

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| kind | yes | `"mouse"` |  |
| event | yes | ["mouseMoved","mousePressed","mouseReleased"] |  |
| x | yes | number | {"minimum":-100000,"maximum":100000} |
| y | yes | number | {"minimum":-100000,"maximum":100000} |
| button | no | ["none","left","middle","right"] | {"default":"left"} |
| clickCount | no | integer | {"default":1,"minimum":0,"maximum":3} |

Example:

```json
{
  "button": "right",
  "event": "mouseMoved",
  "kind": "mouse",
  "x": 0,
  "y": 6
}
```

### key

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| kind | yes | `"key"` |  |
| event | yes | ["keyDown","keyUp","char"] |  |
| key | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| code | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| text | no | string | {"maxLength":256} |
| modifiers | no | integer | {"default":0,"minimum":0,"maximum":15} |

Example:

```json
{
  "event": "keyDown",
  "key": "example",
  "kind": "key",
  "modifiers": 5
}
```

### scroll

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| kind | yes | `"scroll"` |  |
| x | yes | number | {"minimum":-100000,"maximum":100000} |
| y | yes | number | {"minimum":-100000,"maximum":100000} |
| deltaX | yes | number | {"minimum":-100000,"maximum":100000} |
| deltaY | yes | number | {"minimum":-100000,"maximum":100000} |

Example:

```json
{
  "deltaX": 3,
  "deltaY": 6,
  "kind": "scroll",
  "x": 3,
  "y": 4
}
```

### touch

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| kind | yes | `"touch"` |  |
| event | yes | ["touchStart","touchMove","touchEnd","touchCancel"] |  |
| points | yes | array | {"maxItems":10,"items":{"type":"object","properties":{"x":{"type":"number","minimum":-100000,"maximum":100000},"y":{"type":"number","minimum":-100000,"maximum":100000},"id":{"type":"integer","minimum":0,"maximum":9}},"required":["x","y","id"]}} |

Example:

```json
{
  "event": "touchCancel",
  "kind": "touch",
  "points": []
}
```

## BrowserInteractionAction

[JSON Schema](schema/BrowserInteractionAction.json), input validation.

### Variant 1

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| action | yes | `"click"` |  |
| ref | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |

Example:

```json
{
  "action": "click",
  "ref": "example"
}
```

### Variant 2

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| action | yes | `"type"` |  |
| ref | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| text | yes | string | {"maxLength":65536} |

Example:

```json
{
  "action": "type",
  "ref": "example",
  "text": "example"
}
```

### Variant 3

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| action | yes | `"press"` |  |
| key | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| ref | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |

Example:

```json
{
  "action": "press",
  "key": "example"
}
```

### Variant 4

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| action | yes | `"scroll"` |  |
| x | yes | number | {"minimum":-100000,"maximum":100000} |
| y | yes | number | {"minimum":-100000,"maximum":100000} |

Example:

```json
{
  "action": "scroll",
  "x": 4,
  "y": 8
}
```

### Variant 5

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| action | yes | `"drag"` |  |
| ref | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| toRef | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |

Example:

```json
{
  "action": "drag",
  "ref": "example",
  "toRef": "example"
}
```

## BrowserMeasurementOptions

[JSON Schema](schema/BrowserMeasurementOptions.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| observeMs | no | integer | {"default":2000,"minimum":1,"maximum":10000} |
| repeat | no | integer | {"default":1,"minimum":1,"maximum":5} |
| filmstrip | no | boolean | {"default":true} |
| interaction | no | [BrowserInteractionAction.json](schema/BrowserInteractionAction.json) |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "filmstrip": true,
  "repeat": 4
}
```

## BrowserOpen

[JSON Schema](schema/BrowserOpen.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| threadId | yes | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |
| workspaceId | yes | [WorkspaceId.json](schema/WorkspaceId.json) |  |
| profile | no | ["ephemeral","persistent"] |  |
| headed | no | boolean | {"default":false} |
| background | no | boolean | {"default":false} |

Example:

```json
{
  "background": false,
  "headed": true,
  "profile": "persistent",
  "threadId": "example",
  "workspaceId": "example"
}
```

## BrowserOrigin

[JSON Schema](schema/BrowserOrigin.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: An exact canonical HTTP(S) origin, including scheme and optional port, with no path, query, fragment or URL credentials.

Type: string. See JSON Schema for constraints.

Example:

```json
"https://youtube.com"
```

## BrowserOriginBlock

[JSON Schema](schema/BrowserOriginBlock.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| origin | yes | string | {"maxLength":8192} |
| reason | yes | ["approval_required","denied","read_only","timeout","invalid_origin"] |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "origin": "example",
  "reason": "timeout"
}
```

## BrowserOriginGrant

[JSON Schema](schema/BrowserOriginGrant.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| origin | yes | [BrowserOrigin.json](schema/BrowserOrigin.json) |  |
| grantedAt | yes | number |  |
| scope | no | ["thread","page"] |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "grantedAt": 1,
  "origin": "https://youtube.com",
  "scope": "page"
}
```

## BrowserServerMessage

[JSON Schema](schema/BrowserServerMessage.json), input validation.

### browser.backend.lost

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"browser.backend.lost"` |  |
| threadId | yes | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |
| backend | yes | `"embedded"` |  |
| recovery | yes | ["pause","headless"] |  |
| url | yes | string | {"maxLength":8192} |
| pageStateLost | yes | `true` |  |
| reason | yes | string | {"maxLength":2048} |

Example:

```json
{
  "backend": "embedded",
  "pageStateLost": true,
  "reason": "example",
  "recovery": "pause",
  "threadId": "example",
  "type": "browser.backend.lost",
  "url": "example"
}
```

### browser.download.progress

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"browser.download.progress"` |  |
| version | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| phase | yes | ["downloading","verifying","extracting","ready"] |  |
| received | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| total | no | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |

Example:

```json
{
  "phase": "ready",
  "received": 8,
  "type": "browser.download.progress",
  "version": "example"
}
```

### browser.evaluate.grants

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"browser.evaluate.grants"` |  |
| threadId | yes | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |
| grants | yes | array | {"maxItems":256,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/BrowserEvaluateGrant.json"}} |

Example:

```json
{
  "grants": [],
  "threadId": "example",
  "type": "browser.evaluate.grants"
}
```

### browser.result

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"browser.result"` |  |
| requestId | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| ok | yes | boolean |  |
| result | no | any JSON value |  |
| error | no | string | {"maxLength":2048} |
| blocked | no | [BrowserOriginBlock.json](schema/BrowserOriginBlock.json) |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "blocked": {
    "origin": "example",
    "reason": "invalid_origin"
  },
  "ok": false,
  "requestId": "example",
  "result": null,
  "type": "browser.result"
}
```

### browser.state

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"browser.state"` |  |
| state | yes | [BrowserState.json](schema/BrowserState.json) |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "state": {
    "backend": "embedded",
    "blocked": {
      "origin": "example",
      "reason": "timeout"
    },
    "closed": false,
    "controller": "agent",
    "loadError": "example",
    "loading": true,
    "reason": "example",
    "status": "recovering",
    "threadId": "example",
    "url": "example"
  },
  "type": "browser.state"
}
```

### browser.frame

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"browser.frame"` |  |
| threadId | yes | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |
| frame | yes | [BrowserFrame.json](schema/BrowserFrame.json) |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "frame": {
    "data": "example",
    "height": 109,
    "sequence": 4,
    "timestamp": 2,
    "width": 101
  },
  "threadId": "example",
  "type": "browser.frame"
}
```

## BrowserState

[JSON Schema](schema/BrowserState.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| threadId | yes | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |
| controller | yes | ["agent","human","none"] |  |
| owner | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| url | yes | string | {"maxLength":8192} |
| closed | yes | boolean |  |
| backend | no | ["embedded","headless"] |  |
| status | no | ["ready","paused","recovering"] |  |
| reason | no | string | {"maxLength":2048} |
| pageStateLost | no | boolean |  |
| loading | no | boolean |  |
| loadError | no | string | {"maxLength":2048} |
| permissionDenied | no | object | {"properties":{"permission":{"type":"string","maxLength":256},"origin":{"type":"string","maxLength":8192}},"required":["permission","origin"]} |
| blocked | no | [BrowserOriginBlock.json](schema/BrowserOriginBlock.json) |  |
| activeTabId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| tabs | no | array | {"maxItems":8,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/BrowserTab.json"}} |
| downloads | no | array | {"maxItems":128,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/BrowserDownload.json"}} |
| pending_dialog | no | [BrowserDialog.json](schema/BrowserDialog.json) |  |
| takeoverMode | no | ["shared","private"] |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "closed": true,
  "controller": "agent",
  "loading": false,
  "owner": "example",
  "pageStateLost": true,
  "reason": "example",
  "tabs": [],
  "threadId": "example",
  "url": "example"
}
```

## BrowserTab

[JSON Schema](schema/BrowserTab.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| tabId | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| url | yes | string | {"maxLength":8192} |
| title | yes | string | {"maxLength":1024} |
| pending_dialog | no | [BrowserDialog.json](schema/BrowserDialog.json) |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "pending_dialog": {
    "defaultPrompt": "example",
    "dialogId": "example",
    "message": "example",
    "tabId": "example",
    "type": "prompt"
  },
  "tabId": "example",
  "title": "example",
  "url": "example"
}
```

## Capabilities

[JSON Schema](schema/Capabilities.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| permissions | no | [PermissionCapabilities.json](schema/PermissionCapabilities.json) |  |
| permissionModes | no | array | {"maxItems":256,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/NativePermissionMode.json"}} |
| approvals | no | ["interactive","sandbox-only","none"] |  |
| steeringMode | no | ["native","interrupt-restart","queue"] |  |
| forkMode | no | ["native","context-handoff","none"] |  |
| childControls | no | ["native","read-only"] |  |
| childFidelity | no | ["full","summary","placeholder"] |  |
| steer | yes | boolean |  |
| launchOptions | no | array | {"maxItems":2,"items":{"type":"string","enum":["effort","serviceTier"]}} |
| interruptCascades | yes | boolean |  |
| resume | yes | boolean |  |
| fork | yes | boolean |  |
| forkSubagents | no | boolean |  |
| sessionOptions | no | boolean |  |
| forkPoints | no | array | {"maxItems":2,"items":{"type":"string","enum":["turn","item","end"]}} |
| subagentTranscripts | yes | boolean |  |
| backgroundTaskControl | yes | boolean |  |
| backgroundVisibility | yes | ["full","partial","none"] |  |
| planMode | yes | boolean |  |
| tokenUsage | yes | boolean |  |
| imageInput | yes | boolean |  |
| attachmentInput | no | object | {"properties":{"format":{"type":"string","enum":["claude","codex","opencode","acp"]},"documents":{"maxItems":32,"type":"array","items":{"type":"string","maxLength":128}},"embeddedContext":{"type":"boolean"},"maxInlineBytes":{"type":"integer","minimum":0,"maximum":33554432}},"required":["format","documents","embeddedContext","maxInlineBytes"]} |
| rewindFiles | yes | boolean |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "backgroundTaskControl": false,
  "backgroundVisibility": "partial",
  "childControls": "read-only",
  "fork": false,
  "forkMode": "native",
  "forkPoints": [],
  "forkSubagents": true,
  "imageInput": true,
  "interruptCascades": false,
  "launchOptions": [],
  "permissions": {
    "guarantees": [],
    "modes": [],
    "nativeAutoReview": false,
    "toolGate": false
  },
  "planMode": false,
  "resume": false,
  "rewindFiles": false,
  "steer": false,
  "subagentTranscripts": false,
  "tokenUsage": true
}
```

## CatalogChanged

[JSON Schema](schema/CatalogChanged.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| requestId | yes | string |  |
| entries | yes | array | {"maxItems":512,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/CatalogEntry.json"}} |
| stale | yes | boolean |  |
| type | yes | `"catalog.changed"` |  |

Example:

## CatalogEntry

[JSON Schema](schema/CatalogEntry.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| id | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| kind | yes | [CatalogKind.json](schema/CatalogKind.json) |  |
| name | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| title | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| description | yes | string | {"maxLength":2048} |
| icon | no | string | {"maxLength":4096} |
| source | yes | object | {"properties":{"provider":{"anyOf":[{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ProviderKind.json"},{"type":"string","const":"ace"}]},"scope":{"type":"string","enum":["global","project","plugin","ace"]},"path":{"type":"string","maxLength":4096},"plugin":{"type":"string","maxLength":256}},"required":["provider","scope"]} |
| invocation | yes | [CatalogInvocation.json](schema/CatalogInvocation.json) |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "description": "example",
  "id": "example",
  "invocation": {
    "name": "example",
    "type": "slash"
  },
  "kind": "command",
  "name": "example",
  "source": {
    "provider": "ace",
    "scope": "plugin"
  },
  "title": "example"
}
```

## CatalogKind

[JSON Schema](schema/CatalogKind.json), input validation.

Type: ["skill","command","plugin","agent","workflow","mcp-tool","builtin"]. See JSON Schema for constraints.

Example:

```json
"workflow"
```

## CatalogList

[JSON Schema](schema/CatalogList.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: Exactly one of threadId, draft or workspace is required.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| requestId | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":128} |
| threadId | no | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |
| draft | no | object | {"properties":{"draftId":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":128},"workspaceId":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/WorkspaceId.json"},"provider":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ProviderKind.json"},"instanceId":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":128}},"required":["draftId","workspaceId","provider"]} |
| type | yes | `"catalog.list"` |  |
| query | no | string | {"default":"","maxLength":256} |
| limit | no | integer | {"default":100,"minimum":1,"maximum":512} |
| workspace | no | object | {"properties":{"workspaceId":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/WorkspaceId.json"},"provider":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ProviderKind.json"},"instanceId":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":128}},"required":["workspaceId","provider"]} |
| subscribe | no | boolean | {"default":false} |

Example:

```json
{
  "limit": 8,
  "requestId": "example",
  "threadId": "example",
  "type": "catalog.list"
}
```

[JSON Schema](schema/CatalogListResult.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| requestId | yes | string |  |
| entries | yes | array | {"maxItems":512,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/CatalogEntry.json"}} |
| stale | yes | boolean |  |
| type | yes | `"catalog.list.result"` |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "entries": [],
  "requestId": "example",
  "stale": false,
  "type": "catalog.list.result"
}
```

[JSON Schema](schema/CatalogMention.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |

[JSON Schema](schema/CatalogUnsubscribe.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"catalog.unsubscribe"` |  |
| requestId | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":128} |

### events

Semantic rule: throughSeq >= afterSeq; events are ordered with (firstSeq ?? seq) > previous seq and seq <= throughSeq.

| subscriptionId | yes | string |  |

| events | yes | array | {"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/DeliveryEvent.json"}} |

Example:

```json
  "afterSeq": 5,
  "events": [],
  "subscriptionId": "example",
  "throughSeq": 8,
  "type": "events"
}
```

[JSON Schema](schema/DelegationRecord.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| childId | yes | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |
| parentId | yes | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |
| parentAgentId | yes | [AgentId.json](schema/AgentId.json) |  |
| rootId | yes | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |
| requestId | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":128,"pattern":"^[a-zA-Z0-9_.-]+$"} |
| request | yes | [DelegationRequest.json](schema/DelegationRequest.json) |  |
| depth | yes | integer | {"minimum":1,"maximum":8} |
| createdAt | yes | [Timestamp.json](schema/Timestamp.json) |  |
| phase | yes | ["created","running","cancelling","settled"] |  |
| generation | no | integer | {"default":0,"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| outcome | no | [DelegationOutcome.json](schema/DelegationOutcome.json) |  |
| resolvedModel | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| resultDelivery | no | ["parent","owner"] |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "childId": "example",
  "createdAt": 6,
  "depth": 2,
  "generation": 2,
  "parentAgentId": "example",
  "parentId": "example",
  "phase": "created",
  "request": {
    "estimatedLoad": 3,
    "installationId": "example",
    "model": "example",
    "options": {
      "effort": "none"
    },
    "permissionMode": "example",
    "provider": "cursor",
    "requestId": "x_vS3aS-6",
    "role": "example",
    "task": "example",
    "wait": true,
    "waitMs": 5,
    "workspace": {
      "kind": "existing_worktree",
      "path": "example"
    }
  },
  "requestId": "TSyF_l.AE5n",
  "rootId": "example"
}
```

[JSON Schema](schema/InteractionRepeat.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| runs | yes | array | {"minItems":1,"maxItems":5,"items":{"type":"object","properties":{"verdict":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionVerdict.json"},"confidence":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionConfidence.json"}},"required":["verdict","confidence"]}} |
| metrics | yes | object | {"properties":{"refreshHz":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionMetricSummary.json"},"windowMs":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionMetricSummary.json"},"frames":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionMetricSummary.json"},"latencyMs":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionMetricSummary.json"},"settleMs":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionMetricSummary.json"},"effectiveFps":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionMetricSummary.json"},"droppedFrames":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionMetricSummary.json"},"hitchRatioMsPerS":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionMetricSummary.json"},"layoutShift":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionMetricSummary.json"},"hostLoad":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionMetricSummary.json"},"captureOverheadPct":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionMetricSummary.json"},"longTaskMs":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionMetricSummary.json"},"hitchMs":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionMetricSummary.json"}},"required":["refreshHz","windowMs","frames","hitchMs"]} |

Example:

```json
{
  "metrics": {
    "frames": {
      "median": 4,
      "worst": 5
    },
    "hitchMs": {
      "median": 9,
      "worst": 9
    },
    "latencyMs": {
      "median": 3,
      "worst": 4
    },
    "layoutShift": {
      "median": 2,
      "worst": 6
    },
    "longTaskMs": {
      "median": 2,
      "worst": 5
    },
    "refreshHz": {
      "median": 6,
      "worst": 9
    },
    "settleMs": {
      "median": 0,
      "worst": 1
    },
    "windowMs": {
      "median": 5,
      "worst": 7
    }
  },
  "runs": [
    {
      "confidence": "medium",
      "verdict": "janky"
    }
  ]
}
```

## ItemsWindowRequest

[JSON Schema](schema/ItemsWindowRequest.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: Exactly one target; before + after + target is at most 200 items.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"items.window"` |  |
| requestId | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| threadId | yes | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |
| aroundSeq | no | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| itemId | no | [ItemId.json](schema/ItemId.json) |  |
| turnOrdinal | no | integer | {"exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| before | no | integer | {"default":50,"minimum":0,"maximum":199} |
| after | no | integer | {"default":50,"minimum":0,"maximum":199} |

Example:

```json
{
  "after": 5,
  "aroundSeq": 4,
  "before": 3,
  "requestId": "example",
  "threadId": "example",
  "type": "items.window"
}
```

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |

| targetSeq | yes | union | {"anyOf":[{"type":"integer","minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991},{"type":"null"}]} |
| items | yes | array | {"maxItems":200,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/Item.json"}} |
| itemSeqs | yes | object | {"propertyNames":{"type":"string"},"additionalProperties":{"type":"integer","exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991}} |
| itemsBefore | yes | union | {"anyOf":[{"type":"integer","exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991},{"type":"null"}]} |
| itemsAfter | yes | union | {"anyOf":[{"type":"integer","exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991},{"type":"null"}]} |

```json
  "itemsAfter": 9,
```

## PortableHandoff

[JSON Schema](schema/PortableHandoff.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| version | yes | `1` |  |
| sourceThreadId | yes | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |
| origin | no | object | {"properties":{"provider":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ProviderKind.json"},"backend":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":256}},"required":["provider"]} |
| throughSeq | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| lossy | yes | `true` |  |
| policy | yes | `"recent-complete-items"` |  |
| excerpts | yes | array | {"maxItems":200,"items":{"type":"object","properties":{"citation":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/HandoffCitation.json"},"text":{"type":"string","maxLength":8192}},"required":["citation","text"]}} |
| omittedItems | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| history | yes | object | {"properties":{"type":{"type":"string","const":"items.page"},"tool":{"default":"ace_read_handoff","type":"string","const":"ace_read_handoff"},"chunkTool":{"default":"ace_read_handoff_chunk","type":"string","const":"ace_read_handoff_chunk"},"threadId":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ThreadId.json"},"before":{"type":"integer","exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991},"limit":{"type":"number","const":50}},"required":["type","threadId","before","limit"]} |
| limitations | yes | `"Provider-private state is unavailable. Page history for omitted items, full text, reasoning, tools and attachments."` |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "excerpts": [],
  "history": {
    "before": 10,
    "limit": 50,
    "threadId": "example",
    "type": "items.page"
  },
  "limitations": "Provider-private state is unavailable. Page history for omitted items, full text, reasoning, tools and attachments.",
  "lossy": true,
  "omittedItems": 1,
  "policy": "recent-complete-items",
  "sourceThreadId": "example",
  "throughSeq": 1,
  "version": 1
}
```

| port | no | [PreviewPort.json](schema/PreviewPort.json) |  |

Whitespace, Unicode hosts, opaque schemes and parser-normalized URLs are allowed. x-ace-url-minLength and x-ace-url-maxLength apply after trimming whitespace and removing ASCII tab, CR and LF characters, using UTF-16 code units. Install the ace-whatwg-url format validator or enforce this rule in application code."} |

```json
}
```

[JSON Schema](schema/ProjectCloneUrl.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: GitHub owner/repo shorthand, HTTPS, SSH or scp-style git@ URL with a host and repository path; no passwords, HTTPS usernames, whitespace or control bytes.

Type: string. See JSON Schema for constraints.

Example:

```json
"git@example.invalid:repo.git"
```

[JSON Schema](schema/ProjectCommand.json), input validation.

### workspace.add

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"workspace.add"` |  |

| name | no | [ProjectName.json](schema/ProjectName.json) |  |

```json
}
```

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"workspace.create"` |  |

| name | yes | [ProjectName.json](schema/ProjectName.json) |  |
| icon | no | union | {"anyOf":[{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ProjectIcon.json"},{"type":"null"}]} |

| gitignore | no | string | {"maxLength":65536} |

Example:

```json
  "gitignore": "example",
  "icon": "HTTPS://例え.テスト/icon.png",

  "parent": "example",
  "type": "workspace.create"
}
```

### workspace.clone

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"workspace.clone"` |  |

| icon | no | union | {"anyOf":[{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ProjectIcon.json"},{"type":"null"}]} |
| url | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":4096} |

| --- | --- | --- | --- |

```json
  "type": "workspace.update",

}
```

### workspace.rename

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"workspace.rename"` |  |

## ProjectDirectoryName

[JSON Schema](schema/ProjectDirectoryName.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: A literal existing directory name; no forward slash or NUL. Host filesystem rules apply to backslashes.

Type: string. See JSON Schema for constraints.

Example:

```json
"example"
```

## ProjectIcon

[JSON Schema](schema/ProjectIcon.json), input validation.

### Variant 1

Semantic rule: Absolute HTTP or HTTPS WHATWG URL, without ASCII tabs, CR, LF or trailing whitespace; at most 4096 UTF-16 code units. Install the ace-whatwg-url format validator or enforce URL parsing in application code.

Type: string. See JSON Schema for constraints.

Example:

```json
"HTTPS://例え.テスト/icon.png"
```

### Variant 2

Type: string. See JSON Schema for constraints.

Example:

```json
"data:image/webp;base64,a9JpDY44UTYJRh0kJPRfgCn6+BloocE9QoT2A0kJ7A=="
```

## ProviderInstance

[JSON Schema](schema/ProviderInstance.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: Native instances use native account IDs. ACP instances require matching agent, installation and instance references, id equal to instanceId, default_cli home strategy and an empty selector environment.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| id | yes | [AccountInstanceId.json](schema/AccountInstanceId.json) |  |
| implicit | no | `true` |  |
| managed | no | `true` |  |
| acpAgentId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| installationId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| instanceId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| homeStrategy | no | `"default_cli"` |  |
| authMethod | no | ["browser","api_key","unknown"] |  |
| signedInAs | no | string | {"maxLength":256} |
| loginRevision | no | string | {"maxLength":128} |
| profileRevision | no | string | {"maxLength":256} |
| installationVersion | no | string | {"maxLength":256} |
| provider | yes | [AccountProvider.json](schema/AccountProvider.json) |  |
| label | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":128} |
| shortLabel | no | [AccountShortLabel.json](schema/AccountShortLabel.json) |  |
| badgeColor | no | [AccountBadgeColor.json](schema/AccountBadgeColor.json) |  |
| homeDir | yes | [AccountDirectory.json](schema/AccountDirectory.json) |  |
| env | yes | object | {"propertyNames":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/AccountEnvKey.json"},"additionalProperties":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/AccountDirectory.json"}} |

Example:

```json
{
  "env": {
    "CODEX_HOME": "/example/codex"
  },
  "homeDir": "/example/codex",
  "id": "codex-default",
  "label": "Codex",
  "provider": "codex"
}
```

## ReviewAnchor

[JSON Schema](schema/ReviewAnchor.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: Fingerprint lines count equals end - start + 1.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| position | yes | [ReviewPosition.json](schema/ReviewPosition.json) |  |
| revision | yes | [ReviewRevision.json](schema/ReviewRevision.json) |  |
| fingerprint | yes | [ReviewFingerprint.json](schema/ReviewFingerprint.json) |  |
| state | yes | ["active","outdated","addressed-pending-review"] |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "fingerprint": {
    "after": [],
    "before": [],
    "lines": [
      "example"
    ]
  },
  "position": {
    "end": 1,
    "file": "example.ts",
    "side": "new",
    "start": 1
  },
  "revision": {
    "kind": "working-tree"
  },
  "state": "active"
}
```

## ReviewFingerprint

[JSON Schema](schema/ReviewFingerprint.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: Total UTF-16 length of before, lines and after is <= 8192.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| before | yes | array | {"maxItems":3,"items":{"type":"string","maxLength":8192}} |
| lines | yes | array | {"minItems":1,"maxItems":100,"items":{"type":"string","maxLength":8192}} |

```json
}
```

## ReviewPosition

[JSON Schema](schema/ReviewPosition.json), input validation.

| side | yes | ["old","new"] |  |

| end | yes | integer | {"exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":10000000} |

Example:

```json

  "end": 1,
  "file": "example.ts",
  "side": "new",
  "start": 1
}
```

| mode | no | [ScreenMode.json](schema/ScreenMode.json) | {"default":"background"} |
| secureInputAllowed | no | boolean | {"default":false} |
| holder | no | [ScreenAgentScope.json](schema/ScreenAgentScope.json) |  |
| indicator | yes | boolean |  |
| target | yes | [ScreenTarget.json](schema/ScreenTarget.json) |  |

```json
  "capabilities": {
    "capture": {
      "changeDriven": false,
      "displays": false,
      "windows": false
    },
    "codecs": [
      "jpeg"
    ],
    "input": {
      "keyboard": true,
      "pointer": false,
      "scroll": true,
      "text": false
    },
    "permissions": {
      "input": "n/a",
      "screen": "granted"
    },
    "platform": "linux-wayland",
    "semanticActions": [],
    "uiTree": false,
    "version": 2,
    "windowSelection": false
  },
  "controller": "human",
  "indicator": false,
  "lifecycle": "stopped",
  "permissions": {
    "accessibility": false,
    "screenRecording": true
  },

}
```

| role | yes | string | {"maxLength":128} |
| name | yes | string | {"maxLength":256} |
| value | no | string | {"maxLength":512} |
| secondaryActions | no | array | {"maxItems":32,"items":{"type":"string","maxLength":256}} |
| description | no | string | {"maxLength":256} |
| bounds | yes | [ScreenRect.json](schema/ScreenRect.json) |  |
| states | yes | array | {"maxItems":6,"items":{"type":"string","enum":["focused","selected","checked","disabled","expanded","offscreen"]}} |
| actions | yes | array | {"maxItems":8,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ScreenUIAction.json"}} |
| children | yes | array | {"maxItems":512,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ScreenUINode.json"}} |

Example:

```json

  "actions": [

  "bounds": {
    "h": 10,
    "w": 10,
    "x": 0,
    "y": 0
  },
  "children": [],
  "name": "Example",
  "ref": "example",
  "role": "button",
  "states": []
}
```

## SearchFilters

[JSON Schema](schema/SearchFilters.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: after must be <= before when both timestamps are present.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| threadId | no | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |
| workspaceId | no | [WorkspaceId.json](schema/WorkspaceId.json) |  |
| provider | no | [ProviderKind.json](schema/ProviderKind.json) |  |
| after | no | [Timestamp.json](schema/Timestamp.json) |  |
| before | no | [Timestamp.json](schema/Timestamp.json) |  |
| status | no | [SearchState.json](schema/SearchState.json) |  |
| agentId | no | [AgentId.json](schema/AgentId.json) |  |
| kind | no | [SearchKind.json](schema/SearchKind.json) |  |
| kinds | no | array | {"minItems":1,"maxItems":9,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/SearchKind.json"}} |

Example:

```json
{
  "agentId": "example",
  "kind": "tool_call",
  "provider": "acp",
  "status": "needs_you",
  "threadId": "example",
  "workspaceId": "example"
}
```

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| workspaceId | no | [WorkspaceId.json](schema/WorkspaceId.json) |  |
| query | no | string | {"default":"","maxLength":256} |
| cursor | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":2048} |
| limit | no | integer | {"default":10,"minimum":1,"maximum":20} |

Example:

```json
{
  "limit": 1,
  "query": "example",
  "workspaceId": "example"
}
```

## ThreadListView

[JSON Schema](schema/ThreadListView.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| window | no | [ThreadListWindow.json](schema/ThreadListWindow.json) |  |
| kind | yes | `"threads"` |  |
| seq | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| threads | yes | object | {"propertyNames":{"type":"string"},"additionalProperties":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ThreadListEntry.json"},"x-ace-constraint":"Plain JSON object with every own opaque ID, including __proto__, validated and retained."} |

Example:

```json
{
  "kind": "threads",
  "seq": 5,
  "threads": {}
}
```

## ThreadListWindow

[JSON Schema](schema/ThreadListWindow.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| before | yes | union | {"anyOf":[{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ThreadListCursor.json"},{"type":"null"}]} |
| total | yes | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| counts | yes | array | {"items":{"type":"object","properties":{"id":{"type":"string"},"threads":{"type":"integer","minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991}},"required":["id","threads"]}} |

Example:

```json
{
  "before": {
    "at": 3,
    "id": "example"
  },
  "counts": [],
  "total": 4
}
```

## ThreadOrganization

[JSON Schema](schema/ThreadOrganization.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| pinned | no | boolean |  |
| pinOrder | no | number | {"description":"Place among pinned threads, higher first. Set on pin (the daemon's clock when the command names none, so a new pin leads) and cleared on unpin."} |
| unread | no | boolean |  |
| readAt | no | [Timestamp.json](schema/Timestamp.json) |  |
| settledAt | no | [Timestamp.json](schema/Timestamp.json) |  |
| settledReason | no | ["manual","inactivity","pr_merged","pr_closed"] |  |
| snoozedUntil | no | [Timestamp.json](schema/Timestamp.json) |  |
| deletedAt | no | [Timestamp.json](schema/Timestamp.json) |  |
| activityAt | no | [Timestamp.json](schema/Timestamp.json) |  |
| autoSettleAt | no | [Timestamp.json](schema/Timestamp.json) |  |

Example:

```json
{
  "pinned": true,
  "settledAt": 2
}
```

| workspaceId | yes | [WorkspaceId.json](schema/WorkspaceId.json) |  |

| instanceId | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| model | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| accountId | no | [AccountInstanceId.json](schema/AccountInstanceId.json) |  |

```json
  "accountId": "example",
```

```json
}
```

## ThreadRunMetadata

[JSON Schema](schema/ThreadRunMetadata.json), input validation.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| workingSince | no | [Timestamp.json](schema/Timestamp.json) |  |
| provider | no | [ProviderKind.json](schema/ProviderKind.json) |  |
| model | no | string | {"maxLength":256} |
| account | no | string | {"maxLength":256} |
| options | no | [ExecutionOptions.json](schema/ExecutionOptions.json) |  |
| subagentCount | no | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| runningSubagentCount | no | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| backgroundTaskCount | no | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| watching | no | array | {"maxItems":8,"items":{"type":"object","properties":{"id":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/BackgroundTaskId.json"},"title":{"type":"string","maxLength":256}},"required":["id","title"]}} |
| asking | no | array | {"maxItems":8,"items":{"type":"object","properties":{"id":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/InteractionId.json"},"kind":{"type":"string","maxLength":64}},"required":["id","kind"]}} |
| waitingOn | no | integer | {"exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| step | no | ["tests"] |  |
| contextMeter | no | object | {"properties":{"used":{"type":"integer","minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991},"limit":{"anyOf":[{"type":"integer","exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991},{"type":"null"}]},"at":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/Timestamp.json"}},"required":["used","limit","at"]} |

```json
  "waitingOn": 3
}
```

| commands | yes | array | {"maxItems":64,"items":{"type":"object","properties":{"itemId":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/ItemId.json"},"command":{"type":"string","maxLength":1024},"failed":{"type":"boolean"},"exitCode":{"anyOf":[{"type":"integer","minimum":-9007199254740991,"maximum":9007199254740991},{"type":"null"}]}},"required":["itemId","command","failed"]}} |

## TurnsPageRequest

[JSON Schema](schema/TurnsPageRequest.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: At most one exclusive ordinal cursor, before or after.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type | yes | `"turns.page"` |  |
| requestId | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":256} |
| threadId | yes | [ThreadId.json](schema/ThreadId.json) |  |
| before | no | integer | {"exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| after | no | integer | {"exclusiveMinimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
| limit | no | integer | {"default":50,"minimum":1,"maximum":100} |

Example:

```json
{
  "before": 6,
  "limit": 7,
  "requestId": "example",
  "threadId": "example",
  "type": "turns.page"
}
```

## UsageQuery

[JSON Schema](schema/UsageQuery.json), input validation.

Semantic rule: to >= from, their difference is <= 365 days, and groupBy entries are unique.

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| from | yes | string | {"format":"date","pattern":"^(?:(?:\\d\\d[2468][048]\|\\d\\d[13579][26]\|\\d\\d0[48]\|[02468][048]00\|[13579][26]00)-02-29\|\\d{4}-(?:(?:0[13578]\|1[02])-(?:0[1-9]\|[12]\\d\|3[01])\|(?:0[469]\|11)-(?:0[1-9]\|[12]\\d\|30)\|(?:02)-(?:0[1-9]\|1\\d\|2[0-8])))$"} |
| to | yes | string | {"format":"date","pattern":"^(?:(?:\\d\\d[2468][048]\|\\d\\d[13579][26]\|\\d\\d0[48]\|[02468][048]00\|[13579][26]00)-02-29\|\\d{4}-(?:(?:0[13578]\|1[02])-(?:0[1-9]\|[12]\\d\|3[01])\|(?:0[469]\|11)-(?:0[1-9]\|[12]\\d\|30)\|(?:02)-(?:0[1-9]\|1\\d\|2[0-8])))$"} |
| groupBy | no | array | {"default":[],"maxItems":7,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/UsageDimension.json"}} |
| filters | no | object | {"default":{},"propertyNames":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/UsageDimension.json"},"additionalProperties":{"minItems":1,"maxItems":50,"type":"array","items":{"type":"string","minLength":1,"maxLength":8192}}} |
| agentTree | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":512} |
| limit | no | integer | {"default":100,"minimum":1,"maximum":1000} |
| orderBy | no | ["tokens","cost"] | {"default":"tokens"} |
| equivalentApiCost | no | boolean | {"default":false} |
| bucket | no | ["day","week","month"] |  |
| quotaAccount | no | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":512} |

Example:

```json
{
  "from": "2026-10-01",
  "to": "2026-10-02"
}
```

| Field | Required | Type | Constraints |

| timezone | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":512} |
| priceVersion | yes | string | {"minLength":1,"maxLength":512} |
| priceAsOf | no | string | {"format":"date","pattern":"^(?:(?:\\d\\d[2468][048]\|\\d\\d[13579][26]\|\\d\\d0[48]\|[02468][048]00\|[13579][26]00)-02-29\|\\d{4}-(?:(?:0[13578]\|1[02])-(?:0[1-9]\|[12]\\d\|3[01])\|(?:0[469]\|11)-(?:0[1-9]\|[12]\\d\|30)\|(?:02)-(?:0[1-9]\|1\\d\|2[0-8])))$"} |
| priceSources | no | array | {"maxItems":100,"items":{"type":"string","format":"ace-whatwg-url","x-ace-constraint":"Accept strings whose trimmed value is an absolute WHATWG URL. Whitespace, Unicode hosts, opaque schemes and parser-normalized URLs are allowed. x-ace-url-minLength and x-ace-url-maxLength apply after trimming whitespace and removing ASCII tab, CR and LF characters, using UTF-16 code units. Install the ace-whatwg-url format validator or enforce this rule in application code."}} |

| accounts | no | array | {"maxItems":256,"items":{"$ref":"https://ace.local/protocol/v1/AccountSummary.json"}} |

| cachedInputTokens | no | integer | {"minimum":0,"maximum":9007199254740991} |
